+# LSU v3.2.11 — Scope Specification (LQ04 Closure, 11-Model Propagation Audit, RP01 Design)
+
+**Status:** frozen at Task 1 of the v3.2.11 round (2026-11-09).
+**Baseline:** branch `lsu-platform-v3.2.10`, verified tip
+`1394fb499d95e604a5c686572943d6a9884393e1` (delivery record `e11fbf82…` is an
+ancestor; both are on `origin/lsu-platform-v3.2.10`). Baseline binary SHA256
+`f3c2a4423aab81ca565c7adcfb840a7b8f9cdb9f2a6b6b5ecc3d0e148af20694`.
+**Round branch/worktree:** `lsu-platform-v3.2.11` in
+`/home/sdc/gem5-fi-ding/.claude/worktrees/lsu-platform-v3.2.11`.
+**Implementing plan:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-09-lsu-v3.2.11.md`.
+**Baseline evidence:** `docs/gem5-fi/lsu/implementation_v3_2/LSU_v3.2.11_Baseline_Verification.md`.
+
+**Provenance note.** The researcher brief is the authority; it was delivered
+conversationally and is not a repo file. This spec captures its requirements as
+encoded in the implementing plan (whose task headers tag the requirement codes).
+Codes the plan tags directly on tasks — A1–A5, B2, B5–B8, C1–C4 — are quoted from
+those tags; the remaining B-track codes (B1, B3, B4, B9) are the brief's
+cross-cutting audit rules, which the plan encodes in its Global Constraints and
+Review Focus sections; the mapping below states where each is enforced.
+
+---
+
+## 1. Round goal and hard limits
+
+Close the four LQ04 leftovers, independently audit the fault-impact propagation
+chain of all 11 implemented LSU models from raw traces, and complete the RP01
+source audit plus a gated final fault-injection design, then publish the v3.2.11
+Excel, model matrix and unified ZIP.
+
+Hard limits (violations invalidate the round):
+
+1. **No formal experiment is started.** No new Pilot run and no formal run. The
+   only runtime execution permitted in the entire round is **one LQ04 watchdog
+   smoke** after the Task 5 C++ fix, gated on `df -h /home` showing more than
+   **70.6 GiB** free (otherwise record `RUNTIME_NOT_VERIFIED` and skip the
+   runtime part).
+2. **No RP01 C++ implementation.** RP01 is source audit + design only.
+3. **RP01 `DecisionStatus` stays `NEEDS_USER_DECISION`** — never `ACCEPTED`, in
+   every document and every Excel cell of this round.
+4. **Historical artifacts are immutable.** Never overwrite or renumber
+   `pilots_v3_2_*` artifacts, SampleRunIDs, Seeds, or the 60-row identity block.
+   New audit output lives only under `implementation_v3_2/audit_v3_2_11/` (and
+   `rp01_design_v3_2_11/` for RP01).
+5. **Binary purity.** Never mix evidence produced by different binary SHAs.
+   Every pilot row records its binary SHA; a model whose pilots span binaries
+   reports the splits, never a merged verdict.
+6. `ConfigFrozen` stays `NO`; Warm-up/ROI stay `NEEDS_RESEARCHER_CONFIRMATION`.
+7. Disk safety threshold 70.6 GiB free; at Task 1 `df -h /home` shows 86 GiB.
+
+## 2. Task A — LQ04 closure (A1–A5)
+
+- **A1 — Separate structural completeness from fault-effect propagation.**
+  Add `fault_effect_coverage.py` with a `classify(model, events, injection,
+  terminal)` contract returning `fault_effect_propagation_coverage` plus
+  evidence/reason. Keep the existing `propagation_coverage` field unchanged as
+  the *structural* label (aliased to the new `structural_chain_completeness`);
+  add the three `fault_effect_*` fields to `run_pilot.py`'s classification
+  block. LQ04's effect-marker list is empty by design (its effect is the
+  entry-lifetime window), so the LQ04 pilots must grade `LIFECYCLE_ONLY`.
+  Never rewrite historical `pilot_meta.json`. (Plan Tasks 2–3.)
+- **A2 — Correct the LQ04 source line numbers and responsibilities; publish
+  Excel v3.2.6.** Re-derive each `lsq_unit.cc` range from the v3.2.10 source
+  (`insertLoad` 394-464, `executeLoad` 719-831, LQ04 hook 796-828, `lq04Restore`
+  927-943, `commitLoad` 945-973, `commitLoads` 975-987, `squash` 1202-1253),
+  quote the source lines, and correct the workbook row. Frozen fault-definition
+  fields are unchanged. (Plan Tasks 6–7.)
+- **A3 — Fix the watchdog exit path.** The current
+  `exitSimLoop("lq04 watchdog expired", 1)` raises `NotImplementedError` in
+  gem5's stdlib exit-string table. Replace the string with the registered
+  `"m5_fail instruction encountered"` (FAIL event); keep the
+  `CHAOS_LQ04_WATCHDOG_EXPIRED` log line, the stdout line, and the last trace
+  event; then run the one permitted smoke: non-zero exit, no
+  `NotImplementedError`, trace events retained, classification `TIMEOUT`/`HANG`
+  — **never `MASKED`**. (Plan Task 5.)
+- **A4 — Give no-fault controls their own validity semantics.**
+  `run_roles.classify_run_role(probability)` → `CONTROL`/`FAULT`;
+  `control_valid(exit_code, oracle_pass, injection_count, trace_completeness,
+  validator_errors)`; `accepted = fault_valid or control_valid`; a valid control
+  exits 0 with `run_role=CONTROL`, `fault_activation_status=NOT_TRIGGERED`,
+  `oracle_outcome=PASS|FAIL`, and an honest terminal
+  (`UNRESOLVED`/`NO_INJECTION_EVENT`) — a control is **not** a failed fault run
+  and is never `MASKED`. No retro-edit of historical `pilot_meta.json`.
+  (Plan Task 4.)
+- **A5 — Final LQ04 research adjudication.** A frozen verdict document (and the
+  final report section) that re-derives all ten v3.2.10 LQ04 runs (5 fault
+  pilots + 5 controls) from `pilot_meta.json`, `lq04_injections.log`,
+  `trace_v31.jsonl`, `oracle.log`, `stdout.log` and states, verbatim:
+
+  ```text
+  RecordIntegrity=VALID
+  TraceCompleteness=COMPLETE
+  StructuralChainCompleteness=COMPLETE
+  FaultEffectPropagationCoverage=LIFECYCLE_ONLY
+  PROPAGATION_VERIFIED=0
+  ```
+
+  preserving: 5/5 injections, 5/5 oracle PASS, 5/5 MASKED, no response loss, no
+  wrong-data propagation, no application SDC; and stating that the historical
+  `pilot_meta.json` files are not modified and that their
+  `propagation_coverage=COMPLETE` is the structural label only. (Plan Task 3 +
+  final report.)
+
+## 3. Task B — 11-model propagation audit (B1–B9)
+
+**B1 — Audit all 11 implemented models from raw traces.** Independently
+re-derive each model's chain from `trace_v31.jsonl` plus `pilot_meta.json` for
+A01, A03, FW02, SQ01, DA04, LP01, MD04, FW05, SQ03, SP05, LQ04. Historical
+verdicts are inputs to re-derive, never values to copy. (Plan Tasks 8–22, 28.)
+
+**B2 — Streaming audit tool + archived-trace extraction.** `tools/lsu_propagation_audit.py`
+reads `trace_v31.jsonl` line by line (never loading whole 196 MB SQ03 traces),
+exposes `audit_pilot(pilot_dir, model, binary_sha=None)`, `audit_model(model,
+pilot_dirs)`, `write_matrix(rows, out)`. Archived raw traces (SQ01 from the
+v3.2.3 zip, FW05 from the v3.2.7 zip, SQ03 from the v3.2.8 zip, SP05 from the
+v3.2.9 zip) are extracted only into `audit_v3_2_11/extracted_evidence/`, never
+over `pilots_v3_2_*`, and every extracted trace is hash-verified against the
+recorded checksums (mismatch ⇒ `missing=HASH_MISMATCH`). (Plan Tasks 8–9.)
+
+**B3 — Time adjacency is not causality.** A chain edge exists only from an
+explicit `ptag`, `dyn_inst_seq`, or data-hash link. Injection plus an unrelated
+adjacent event with no link must grade `PARTIAL`/`UNRESOLVED`, never
+`COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN`. (Plan Tasks 2, 8; Review Focus 2.)
+
+**B4 — Historical artifacts untouched.** A report or matrix step that writes
+into `pilots_v3_2_*` invalidates the evidence chain. Extraction steps verify
+`sha256sum` before/after and the final check asserts `git status --short` has
+no `pilots_v3_2_*` entry. (Plan Tasks 9, 21; Review Focus 1.)
+
+**B5 — One audit report per model.** Eleven reports under
+`audit_v3_2_11/<MODEL>_Propagation_Audit_v3.2.11.md`, one commit each, in the
+order A01, A03, FW02, SQ01, DA04, LP01, MD04, FW05, SQ03, SP05, LQ04. Every
+report uses the same template: identity/provenance table (per pilot:
+SampleRunID, seed, ordinal, binary SHA, workload SHA, oracle); seven-stage
+table (one row per pilot); quoted primary evidence (`file:line`, event id,
+hashes); terminal causality (observed terminal vs fault causality, stated
+separately); oracle comparison; missing-evidence list; `FinalClassification`;
+supplementary-pilot recommendation with B8 justification fields (or `none`).
+(Plan Tasks 10–20.)
+
+**B6 — Seven-stage audit contract with explicit missing codes.** The seven
+chain stages — `injection`, `activation`, `early anomaly`, `intermediate
+propagation`, `register writeback`, `consumer`, `commit` — plus the two
+adjudication fields `terminal` and `oracle`, each recorded as
+`injection_verified`, `activation_verified`, `early_anomaly_verified`,
+`intermediate_propagation_verified`, `register_writeback_verified`,
+`consumer_verified`, `commit_verified`, `terminal_verified`, `oracle_verified`
+with values `yes|no|n/a|missing`. `missing` is a list of explicit reason codes
+(`NO_INJECTION_EVENT`, `NO_CONSUMER_EDGE`, `HASH_MISMATCH`, `TRACE_INCOMPLETE`,
+`BINARY_SHA_MISMATCH`, `PILOT_DIR_ABSENT`, …). Never emit a blank or a bare
+`PASS`; no `PASS`/`COMPLETE` without evidence. Expected paths are derived from
+each model's frozen Excel sheet-4 definition, not copied from a list.
+(Plan Task 8 + Tasks 10–20.)
+
+**B7 — Unified audit matrix.** `audit_v3_2_11/LSU_11Model_Propagation_Audit_Matrix.csv`
+with exactly the 20 enumerated columns: `ModelID, SubUnit, FaultDefinition,
+PilotCount, InjectionVerified, ActivationVerified, EarlyAnomalyVerified,
+IntermediatePropagationVerified, RegisterWritebackVerified, ConsumerVerified,
+CommitVerified, TerminalVerified, OracleVerified, StructuralChainCompleteness,
+FaultEffectPropagationCoverage, FaultCausality, FinalClassification,
+MissingEvidence, SupplementaryPilotRequired, EvidencePaths`; 11 data rows; no
+cell empty or a bare `PASS`; the LQ04 row is `LIFECYCLE_ONLY`/`0`; the
+`PROPAGATION_VERIFIED` count is derived from verdicts, never copied. (SDD
+ruling: the plan text's "19-column" is a typo; the enumerated 20 columns are
+binding.) (Plan Task 21.)
+
+**B8 — Supplementary-pilot plan, approval-gated.** For each model needing
+supplementary evidence: the missing event/evidence, why the current trace
+cannot supply it, the hook/workload change required, the minimal run count, the
+preserved SampleRunID/Seed, and the non-overwrite rule. Every item is marked
+`AWAITING_RESEARCHER_APPROVAL`; **nothing is executed**. If no gap needs a run,
+state `none`. (Plan Task 22.)
+
+**B9 — Binary purity.** The audit refuses to compare a pilot's recorded binary
+SHA with a different audit binary (`BINARY_SHA_MISMATCH`). Models whose pilots
+span several binaries get one row per binary split labelled `<MODEL>@<sha8>`
+plus a model-level row stating `MIXED_BINARY_SPLIT` — never a merged verdict.
+(Plan Tasks 8, 21; Review Focus 5.)
+
+### 3.1 The four coverage classes (frozen definitions)
+
+| Class | Definition |
+|---|---|
+| `COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN` | A value divergence (`data_corruption=1`, `consumer_input_matches_source=0`, or `consumer_input_fnv != source_value_hash`, or a model effect marker with a downstream linked event) **plus** a downstream commit/terminal explanation exists |
+| `PARTIAL` | A divergence or effect marker exists but without the downstream link |
+| `LIFECYCLE_ONLY` | The injection changed state but nothing diverged (LQ04's target class) |
+| `UNRESOLVED` | No injection event, or the trace is incomplete |
+
+## 4. Task C — RP01 source audit and gated design (C1–C4)
+
+- **C1 — Source audit of the normal replay path and failure modes.** Verified
+  source tables for `LSQUnit::read` (`lsq_unit.cc:1641-2075`), the
+  partial-coverage branch (`1990-2030`), `rescheduleMemInst` call (`2016`),
+  strictly-ordered branch (`1657-1680`, call `1665`), `IEW::rescheduleMemInst`
+  (`iew.cc:562-566`), `InstructionQueue::rescheduleMemInst`
+  (`inst_queue.cc:1322-1333`), `MemDepUnit::reschedule`/`replay`
+  (`mem_dep_unit.cc:380-384`, `386-…`), `DynInst::executionAttemptId/
+  replayGeneration` (`dyn_inst.hh:353-355`), `stallingLoadIdx`
+  (`lsq_unit.hh:565`) — each re-verified with `grep -n` and quoted. Answers to
+  Question A (why replay happens: partial store→load overlap →
+  `PartialAddrRangeCoverage` → stall record → `++replayGeneration` →
+  `rescheduleMemInst` → `instsToReplay` → `MemDepUnit::replay` → IQ wake →
+  re-execute → full forwarding → commit), Question B (skip one reschedule:
+  reason from the bodies — the load stays in the IQ, `stalled`/
+  `stallingLoadIdx` remain, `MemDepUnit::replay()` cannot recover an inst never
+  pushed to `instsToReplay`, the request is already discarded — concluding with
+  the concrete predicted terminal: HANG vs replayed vs wrong data), and
+  Question D (safety: permanent wait, LQ entry never freed, IQ resource
+  exhaustion, duplicate scheduling, duplicate writeback, wrong commit, internal
+  state corruption; internal watchdog + external timeout; a timeout saves
+  partial trace and is never `MASKED`). (Plan Task 23.)
+- **C2 — Workload verification.** Verify `replay_dependency` existence, SHA256
+  (`ca15390f1a797c55074703dc3fe10d1e07f7704794a9abca96b2761c41d838f4` at plan
+  time), golden oracle (`59fc61c040618747`), ELF type; reproduce the oracle on
+  the host; confirm the partial store→load overlap with an observable consumer
+  from source. Per the SDD preflight ruling this uses existing traces and
+  source only — **no new gem5 execution**; if existing evidence cannot prove
+  replay events, record `WORKLOAD_UNVERIFIED_PENDING_COUNT_ONLY` and defer the
+  count-only preflight to researcher approval. If the workload cannot produce a
+  partial-forward event, report `WORKLOAD_INSUFFICIENT` and do not invent a
+  replacement. (Plan Task 25.)
+- **C3 — Candidate comparison and five dynamic contexts.** Compare candidates
+  A (partial-forward replay loss at `lsq_unit.cc:2016`), B (strict-order replay
+  loss at `1657-1680`) and C (violation/snoop replay loss at the `replayMemInst`
+  sites `1357`, `1496`) on real source hook, independent fault semantics,
+  implementability, distinct propagation signature, overlap with MD04 (MD04
+  suppresses a violation return at a different site; RP01 would remove a replay
+  trigger), workload fit, and unrecoverable-HANG risk. Design five distinct
+  eligible-event contexts preserving the frozen identities
+  (`RP01-F6-W7-S01-R01..R05`, seeds `3135797961`, `226489823`, `1735144973`,
+  `2455796178`, `3984724524`, ordinals 1–5), each distinguishable before
+  inspecting the outcome — no post-hoc selection by SDC, no five reruns of one
+  context. The count-only preflight is stated as a future step awaiting
+  approval, not executed. (Plan Tasks 24, 26.)
+- **C4 — Final design gate.** Twelve required items (fault definition, exact
+  injection site, normal path, fault path, safety analysis, HANG risk, MD04
+  difference, workload verification, five contexts, expected chain, oracle
+  design, recommendation); exactly one declaration of `IMPLEMENTABLE` /
+  `IMPLEMENTABLE_WITH_CONDITIONS` / `NOT_SAFE_TO_IMPLEMENT` with every
+  condition listed verbatim; `DecisionStatus` remains `NEEDS_USER_DECISION`.
+  (Plan Task 27.)
+
+## 5. Version and Excel rules
+
+1. The round publishes **Excel v3.2.6**, built from v3.2.5 by a committed
+   builder tool (`tools/build_lsu_v326_workbook.py`), never by hand-editing the
+   binary workbook.
+2. First build changes **only** the LQ04 sheet-4 row (corrected function names,
+   line numbers, Evidence, SourceAuditStatus) and appends the sheet
+   `19.v3.2.11变更记录`. Later tasks regenerate the same file with the audit
+   and RP01 results (sheet 3 notes, sheet 5 statuses where the audit changes a
+   verdict, new sheet-19 rows).
+3. The 60-row identity block (SampleRunID/RunID/SubTaskID/Seed) is
+   **byte-identical** to v3.2.5. `ConfigFrozen` is `NO` in every row; Warm-up
+   and ROI cells are `NEEDS_RESEARCHER_CONFIRMATION`. No other model row changes
+   in the first build.
+4. `MODEL_STATUS_MATRIX.csv` is updated only from the Task 21 verdicts:
+   `PROPAGATION_VERIFIED=1` only where the audit produced
+   `COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN`; LQ04 stays `0`; RP01 keeps `DESIGNED=1,
+   SOURCE_AUDITED=1, IMPLEMENTED=0…` with an evidence pointer to its report.
+5. Round outputs and version numbers never renumber or overwrite history: new
+   evidence lives only under `audit_v3_2_11/`, `rp01_design_v3_2_11/` and
+   `logs_v3_2_11/`.
+
+## 6. Git discipline
+
+1. One patch per unit: one commit per plan task; solve tasks sequentially, never
+   batched.
+2. Stage explicit paths only. **Never** `git add -A`, `git reset --hard`, or
+   `git clean -fd`.
+3. Push every verified commit; first push is `git push -u origin
+   lsu-platform-v3.2.11`; never force-push; never push to `main`.
+4. Self-verification before commit is mandatory and real: build clean (for the
+   one C++ change), run the affected behavior with real commands, quote real
+   output, run at least one unaffected regression test.
+5. The main `fi-ding` worktree's pre-existing deletions/untracked files and the
+   v3.2.10 worktree's `.tmp_lsu_conc/` are not this round's business and must
+   not be touched.
+
+## 7. Delivery (Section E of the final ZIP)
+
+The round ends with `GIT_COMMIT_v3211.txt`, `GIT_STATUS_v3211.txt`,
+`GIT_DIFF_v3211.txt`, `DELIVERABLES_SHA256_v3211.txt`,
+`LSU_v3.2.11_LQ04_Closure_11Model_Propagation_Audit_RP01_Design_Package.zip`
+(+ `.sha256`), and `LSU_v3.2.11_Final_Report.md` answering the brief's twelve
+questions from this round's data, including the derived count of verified
+models/pilots, the SDC/MASKED/SQUASHED/CRASH breakdown, the RP01
+recommendation, and `Formal experiment = NOT_STARTED`.
+
+## 8. Non-goals
+
+- No new Pilot or formal run (only the Task 5 watchdog smoke may execute gem5).
+- No RP01 C++ implementation; RP01 never leaves the design gate this round.
+- No change to the 12 models' frozen fault definitions, workloads, SampleRunIDs
+  or Seeds.
+- No change to `ConfigFrozen=NO` or Warm-up/ROI `NEEDS_RESEARCHER_CONFIRMATION`.
+- No merging of evidence across binary SHAs.
+- No retro-edit of any historical document, `pilot_meta.json` or pilot artifact
+  under `pilots_v3_2_*`.

diff --git a/tests/test_fault_effect_coverage.py b/tests/test_fault_effect_coverage.py
new file mode 100644
index 00000000..bc5f74d9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_fault_effect_coverage.py
@@ -0,0 +1,495 @@
+"""Fault-effect propagation coverage tests (LSU v3.2.11 Task 2 / A1).
+
+Structural chain completeness (``trace_completeness.propagation_coverage``)
+answers "did the expected stages appear on the fault's own chain".  The
+classifier tested here answers the different question "did the fault's
+EFFECT actually propagate": a trace can be structurally complete while the
+injected fault never diverged any value or control decision (LQ04 is the
+canonical case -- its effect is the entry-lifetime window, and the delivered
+data is unchanged by construction).
+
+The synthetic event lists mirror the real v3.1 JSONL schema: ``record_type``
+``event``/``summary`` records, semicolon-separated ``detail`` key=value
+strings, and ``source_event_id`` linking a consumer to its producer.
+Numeric identity fields appear as ints in some trace vintages and as JSON
+strings in others, so every identity comparison must go through ``str()``
+normalization (covered by the mixed-type tests below).
+"""
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+PLATFORM = ROOT / "docs/gem5-fi/lsu/implementation_v3_2/platform"
+sys.path.insert(0, str(PLATFORM))
+
+from fault_effect_coverage import EFFECT_MARKERS, classify  # noqa: E402
+from trace_completeness import check_completeness  # noqa: E402
+from trace_validator import validate  # noqa: E402
+from trace_writer import TraceWriter  # noqa: E402
+import run_pilot  # noqa: E402
+
+LQ04_HASH = "c6d4496e17859d5"
+MD04_SOURCE_HASH = "aa11aa11aa11aa11"
+MD04_CONSUMER_HASH = "bb22bb22bb22bb22"
+
+
+def _ev(event_id, event_type, ptag, seq, detail="", **extra):
+    rec = {"record_type": "event", "event_id": event_id,
+           "event_type": event_type, "ptag": ptag, "dyn_inst_seq": seq,
+           "detail": detail}
+    rec.update(extra)
+    return rec
+
+
+def _summary(terminal, completeness="COMPLETE", model="LQ04",
+             activation="TRIGGERED"):
+    return {"record_type": "summary", "fault_model_id": model,
+            "fault_activation_status": activation, "terminal_state": terminal,
+            "trace_completeness": completeness}
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Synthetic chains (brief Step 1 assertions)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+lq04_injection = _ev(
+    3, "injection", "1", "3",
+    detail="site=executeLoad_post;family=lq04;field=valid+request"
+           ";valid_before=1;valid_after=0;request_before=1;request_after=0"
+           ";lq_idx=1;lq_gen=1;request_id=3;injected=1;data_unchanged=1",
+    injection_status="INJECTED", activation_status="TRIGGERED")
+
+# LQ04 with the correct payload on every consumer edge: the chain is
+# structurally complete (response -> writeback -> consumers -> commit) but
+# nothing ever diverged.
+lq04_correct_events = [
+    _ev(1, "lq_lifecycle", "0", "3",
+        detail="queue=LQ;action=alloc;index=1;generation=1;reason=insert"),
+    lq04_injection,
+    _ev(8, "memory_response", "1", "3",
+        detail=f"family=lq04;data_fnv={LQ04_HASH};request_id=1;response_id=1"),
+    _ev(9, "register_writeback", "1", "3", source_event_id=8,
+        detail=f"family=lq04;data_corruption=0;writeback_value_fnv={LQ04_HASH}"
+               f";payload_fnv={LQ04_HASH};value_matches_payload=1"
+               f";source_value_hash={LQ04_HASH}"),
+    _ev(10, "commit", "0", "3", detail="trigger=fault_instruction_commit"),
+    _ev(11, "errant_commit", "1", "3", detail="trigger=faulting_load_commit"),
+    _ev(18, "consumer_commit", "1", "8", source_event_id=9,
+        detail=f"source_value_hash={LQ04_HASH};consumer_input_fnv={LQ04_HASH}"
+               f";consumer_input_matches_source=1"),
+    _summary("MASKED", model="LQ04"),
+]
+
+md04_injection = _ev(
+    5, "injection", "1", "7",
+    detail="site=LSQUnit::checkViolations;family=md04;load_seq=7;store_seq=5"
+           ";violation_report_suppressed=1",
+    injection_status="INJECTED", activation_status="TRIGGERED")
+
+# A consumer edge whose input hash differs from the source hash.
+md04_diverged_events = [
+    md04_injection,
+    _ev(30, "register_writeback", "1", "7", source_event_id=5,
+        detail=f"family=md04;writeback_value_fnv={MD04_SOURCE_HASH}"
+               f";source_value_hash={MD04_SOURCE_HASH}"),
+    _ev(31, "consumer_commit", "1", "9", source_event_id=30,
+        detail=f"source_value_hash={MD04_SOURCE_HASH}"
+               f";consumer_input_fnv={MD04_CONSUMER_HASH}"
+               f";consumer_input_matches_source=0"),
+    _summary("MASKED", model="MD04"),
+]
+
+fw02_injection = _ev(
+    2, "injection", "1", "5",
+    detail="site=lsq_fwd_decision;family=addrpath;is_load=1;old_size=8"
+           ";new_size=8;old_addr=7ffffefc28;new_addr=7ffffefc28",
+    injection_status="INJECTED", activation_status="TRIGGERED")
+
+# Injection plus an unrelated time-adjacent event: different ptag AND
+# different dyn_inst_seq, so no chain identity links it.  Its diverged-looking
+# writeback must never be counted as fault-effect evidence.
+adjacent_unlinked_events = [
+    fw02_injection,
+    _ev(3, "register_writeback", "2", "9", timestamp=1001,
+        detail="family=addrpath;data_corruption=1;writeback_value_fnv=dead"
+               ";value_matches_payload=0"),
+    _summary("UNRESOLVED", model="FW02"),
+]
+
+events_without_injection = [
+    _ev(1, "sq_lifecycle", "0", "18",
+        detail="queue=SQ;action=alloc;index=1;generation=1;reason=insert"),
+    _ev(2, "store_mem_write", "1", "18",
+        detail="family=store_data;before_hex=00;after_hex=08"),
+    _summary("UNRESOLVED", model="SQ01"),
+]
+
+
+def test_lq04_correct_payload_is_lifecycle_only():
+    result = classify("LQ04", lq04_correct_events, lq04_injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["control_divergence"] == []
+
+
+def test_md04_consumer_hash_mismatch_is_complete_causal_chain():
+    result = classify("MD04", md04_diverged_events, md04_injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN"
+    kinds = {d["kind"] for d in
+             result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"]}
+    assert "consumer_input_matches_source" in kinds
+    assert "consumer_input_fnv_mismatch" in kinds
+
+
+def test_fw02_adjacent_unlinked_event_is_never_complete():
+    result = classify("FW02", adjacent_unlinked_events, fw02_injection,
+                      "UNRESOLVED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] in ("PARTIAL",
+                                                           "UNRESOLVED")
+    # The time-adjacent diverged writeback is not on the fault's chain:
+    # the chain is the injection alone, and no divergence was credited.
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["chain_events"] == 1
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+
+
+def test_no_injection_event_is_unresolved():
+    result = classify("SQ01", events_without_injection, None, "UNRESOLVED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "UNRESOLVED"
+    assert result["fault_effect_reason"] == "NO_INJECTION_EVENT"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# str() normalization of numeric identity fields
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_mixed_int_str_identity_fields_still_link_the_chain():
+    # Injection ptag is an int while the chained events carry strings (and
+    # the injection seq is a string while the writeback carries an int); the
+    # consumer's source_event_id is an int pointing at a string event_id.
+    # Without str() normalization the chain would be empty and the hash
+    # mismatch invisible.
+    injection = {"record_type": "event", "event_id": "5",
+                 "event_type": "injection", "ptag": 1, "dyn_inst_seq": "7",
+                 "injection_status": "INJECTED",
+                 "activation_status": "TRIGGERED",
+                 "detail": "family=md04;violation_report_suppressed=1"}
+    events = [
+        injection,
+        {"record_type": "event", "event_id": "30",
+         "event_type": "register_writeback", "ptag": "1", "dyn_inst_seq": 7,
+         "source_event_id": "5",
+         "detail": f"writeback_value_fnv={MD04_SOURCE_HASH}"},
+        {"record_type": "event", "event_id": 31,
+         "event_type": "consumer_commit", "ptag": "1", "dyn_inst_seq": "9",
+         "source_event_id": 30,
+         "detail": f"consumer_input_fnv={MD04_CONSUMER_HASH}"
+                   f";consumer_input_matches_source=1"},
+        _summary("MASKED", model="MD04"),
+    ]
+    result = classify("MD04", events, injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["chain_events"] == 3
+    # matches_source=1, so only the fnv comparison exposes the divergence.
+    kinds = {d["kind"] for d in
+             result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"]}
+    assert kinds == {"consumer_input_fnv_mismatch"}
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN"
+
+
+def test_mixed_types_never_fabricate_divergence():
+    # Same mixed-type chain as above but with every hash agreeing: the type
+    # mismatch itself must not be read as a value divergence.
+    injection = {"record_type": "event", "event_id": "3",
+                 "event_type": "injection", "ptag": 1, "dyn_inst_seq": "3",
+                 "injection_status": "INJECTED",
+                 "activation_status": "TRIGGERED",
+                 "detail": "family=lq04;data_unchanged=1"}
+    events = [
+        injection,
+        {"record_type": "event", "event_id": "9",
+         "event_type": "register_writeback", "ptag": "1", "dyn_inst_seq": 3,
+         "source_event_id": "3",
+         "detail": f"data_corruption=0;value_matches_payload=1"
+                   f";writeback_value_fnv={LQ04_HASH}"},
+        {"record_type": "event", "event_id": 18,
+         "event_type": "consumer_commit", "ptag": "1", "dyn_inst_seq": "8",
+         "source_event_id": 9,
+         "detail": f"consumer_input_fnv={LQ04_HASH}"
+                   f";consumer_input_matches_source=1"},
+        _summary("MASKED", model="LQ04"),
+    ]
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Consumer->consumer re-taint edges (Task 2 review fix)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+# Real-trace shape (verified on pilots_v3_2_10/LQ04-R01): 325 of the 326
+# chained consumer edges are consumer->consumer re-taints (their source is
+# itself a consumer_commit whose source hash lives in consumer_output_fnv;
+# the remaining edge sources the register_writeback), and every
+# consumer_commit carries its own source_value_hash next to its
+# consumer_input_fnv.
+RETAINT_IN_HASH = "1a2b3c4d5e6f7a8b"   # value the first consumer read
+RETAINT_OUT_HASH = "9988776655443322"  # value the first consumer produced
+RETAINT_BAD_HASH = "deadbeefdeadbeef"  # corrupted value read downstream
+
+retaint_injection = _ev(
+    4, "injection", "1", "4",
+    detail="site=executeLoad_post;family=lq04;valid_before=1"
+           ";valid_after=0",
+    injection_status="INJECTED", activation_status="TRIGGERED")
+
+
+def _retaint_events(first_output_fnv, second_consumer_detail):
+    """Injection -> writeback -> consumer 40 -> consumer 41.
+
+    The edge into event 41 is a consumer->consumer re-taint edge;
+    ``first_output_fnv`` is the consumer_output_fnv recorded by the source
+    consumer (event 40).
+    """
+    return [
+        retaint_injection,
+        _ev(9, "register_writeback", "1", "4", source_event_id=8,
+            detail=f"family=lq04;data_corruption=0;value_matches_payload=1"
+                   f";writeback_value_fnv={RETAINT_IN_HASH}"),
+        _ev(40, "consumer_commit", "1", "9", source_event_id=9,
+            detail=f"source_value_hash={RETAINT_IN_HASH}"
+                   f";consumer_input_fnv={RETAINT_IN_HASH}"
+                   f";consumer_output_fnv={first_output_fnv}"
+                   f";consumer_input_matches_source=1"),
+        _ev(41, "consumer_commit", "1", "10", source_event_id=40,
+            detail=second_consumer_detail),
+        _summary("MASKED", model="LQ04"),
+    ]
+
+
+def test_retaint_edge_own_source_value_hash_mismatch_diverges():
+    # The second consumer's own re-taint bookkeeping (source_value_hash)
+    # disagrees with the value it actually read (consumer_input_fnv).  The
+    # source consumer's output hash agrees with the read and every
+    # consumer_input_matches_source flag is 1, so ONLY the own-hash signal
+    # fires -- before the review fix this divergence was invisible.
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        RETAINT_BAD_HASH,
+        f"source_value_hash={RETAINT_OUT_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_fnv={RETAINT_BAD_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    diverged = result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"]
+    assert diverged
+    assert {d["kind"] for d in diverged} == {"source_value_hash_mismatch"}
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == \
+        "COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN"
+
+
+def test_retaint_edge_source_consumer_output_fnv_mismatch_diverges():
+    # The value diverged between the source consumer's output and the
+    # downstream consumer's input, while the downstream bookkeeping
+    # (source_value_hash) followed the corrupted value: only the source's
+    # consumer_output_fnv exposes the divergence.  Before the review fix
+    # that key was missing from _SOURCE_HASH_KEYS, so the source-hash
+    # comparison was inert on consumer->consumer edges.
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        RETAINT_OUT_HASH,
+        f"source_value_hash={RETAINT_BAD_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_fnv={RETAINT_BAD_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    diverged = result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"]
+    assert diverged
+    assert {d["kind"] for d in diverged} == {"consumer_input_fnv_mismatch"}
+    assert all(d["source_hash_kind"] == "consumer_output_fnv"
+               for d in diverged)
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == \
+        "COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN"
+
+
+def test_retaint_edge_matching_hashes_do_not_diverge():
+    # The intact re-taint edge: the second consumer's own hashes agree and
+    # match the source consumer's output -- no divergence may be fabricated
+    # (the real LQ04-R01 trace is 325 such edges plus one writeback edge,
+    # and must grade LIFECYCLE_ONLY).
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        RETAINT_OUT_HASH,
+        f"source_value_hash={RETAINT_OUT_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_fnv={RETAINT_OUT_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Converter null hash sentinels (Task 2 fix loop, second finding)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+# Real-trace shape (verified on pilots_v3_2_5/LP01-R01): the converter writes
+# the LITERAL STRING "null" into hash fields it has no value for, and older
+# vintages write "0" (e.g. forward_data_fnv=0).  LP01 class=cc consumer
+# nodes carry source_value_hash=null / consumer_output_fnv=null next to a
+# real consumer_input_fnv.  Truthiness-only guards read those sentinels as
+# real hashes and fabricated 204 null-artifact evidence items archive-wide
+# (LP01-R01: 96 items -- every single hash-based item on that trace), so
+# every hash comparison must first normalize the field to a real hash or
+# ABSENT; divergence must never be fabricated from an absent hash.
+SENTINEL_REAL_HASH = "0f1e2d3c4b5a6978"  # real hash for the sentinel tests
+
+
+def test_null_consumer_input_fnv_against_real_source_hash_does_not_diverge():
+    # (a) consumer_input_fnv="null" while the own-edge bookkeeping and the
+    # source event both carry real hashes: "null" is a sentinel, not a hash,
+    # so neither the own-edge check nor the source-hash lookup may compare
+    # it against anything.
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        RETAINT_OUT_HASH,
+        f"source_value_hash={SENTINEL_REAL_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_fnv=null"
+        f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+
+def test_both_null_hashes_do_not_diverge():
+    # (b) the converter wrote "null" on both sides of the edge.  The
+    # own-edge check compares equal sentinels anyway; the source-hash
+    # lookup compared the sentinel consumer_input_fnv against the source's
+    # real consumer_output_fnv and fabricated a mismatch before the fix.
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        RETAINT_OUT_HASH,
+        "source_value_hash=null;consumer_input_fnv=null"
+        ";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+
+def test_zero_sentinel_hashes_do_not_diverge():
+    # (c) the "0" sentinel (seen in real traces as forward_data_fnv=0), on
+    # the consumer side (own-edge guard) and on the source side (source-hash
+    # lookup).
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        RETAINT_OUT_HASH,
+        f"source_value_hash={SENTINEL_REAL_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_fnv=0;consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        "0",
+        f"source_value_hash={RETAINT_OUT_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_fnv={RETAINT_OUT_HASH}"
+        f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+
+def test_real_differing_hashes_still_diverge():
+    # (d) positive control for the sentinel normalization: two REAL
+    # differing hashes must keep diverging through both guards, and no
+    # sentinel value may leak into the recorded evidence.
+    events = _retaint_events(
+        "7777000044448888",
+        f"source_value_hash=aaaabbbbccccdddd"
+        f";consumer_input_fnv=1111222233334444"
+        f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+    diverged = result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"]
+    assert {d["kind"] for d in diverged} == {"source_value_hash_mismatch",
+                                             "consumer_input_fnv_mismatch"}
+    for d in diverged:
+        for key in ("consumer_input_fnv", "source_value_hash", "source_hash"):
+            value = d.get(key)
+            if value is not None:
+                assert str(value).strip().lower() not in ("null", "none", "",
+                                                          "0")
+    assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == \
+        "COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN"
+
+
+def test_none_and_empty_sentinel_hashes_do_not_diverge():
+    # Completes the sentinel family: the Python-style "None" string and the
+    # empty field are absent hashes on either side of both guards.
+    for sentinel in ("None", ""):
+        events = _retaint_events(
+            RETAINT_OUT_HASH,
+            f"source_value_hash={SENTINEL_REAL_HASH}"
+            f";consumer_input_fnv={sentinel}"
+            f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+        result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+        assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+
+        events = _retaint_events(
+            sentinel,
+            f"source_value_hash={RETAINT_OUT_HASH}"
+            f";consumer_input_fnv={RETAINT_OUT_HASH}"
+            f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+        result = classify("LQ04", events, retaint_injection, "MASKED")
+        assert result["fault_effect_evidence"]["value_divergence"] == []
+        assert result["fault_effect_propagation_coverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Markers, platform fields and wiring
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_effect_markers_cover_test_models_and_lq04_is_empty():
+    for model in ("LQ04", "MD04", "FW02", "SQ01"):
+        assert model in EFFECT_MARKERS
+    # LQ04's effect is the entry-lifetime window itself: no control marker
+    # exists, so LQ04 pilots must grade LIFECYCLE_ONLY when data is intact.
+    assert EFFECT_MARKERS["LQ04"] == ()
+
+
+def test_structural_chain_completeness_alias(tmp_path):
+    path = tmp_path / "trace_v31.jsonl"
+    w = TraceWriter(path, "PILOT-LQ04", "PILOT-LQ04", "PILOT-LQ04-S01",
+                    "LQ04", "LQ04")
+    w.event("injection", ptag=41, dyn_inst_seq=7, timestamp=100,
+            injection_status="INJECTED", activation_status="TRIGGERED",
+            trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+            detail="site=executeLoad_post;family=lq04;valid_before=1"
+                   ";valid_after=0")
+    resp = w.event("memory_response", ptag=41, dyn_inst_seq=7, timestamp=200,
+                   activation_status="TRIGGERED",
+                   trace_completeness="COMPLETE", request_id=7, response_id=1,
+                   detail=f"family=lq04;data_fnv={LQ04_HASH};size=8")
+    wb = w.event("register_writeback", ptag=41, dyn_inst_seq=7, timestamp=300,
+                 activation_status="TRIGGERED",
+                 trace_completeness="COMPLETE", new_value="5f5e5d5c",
+                 source_event_id=resp,
+                 detail=f"family=lq04;data_corruption=0"
+                        f";writeback_value_fnv={LQ04_HASH}")
+    w.event("consumer_commit", ptag=41, dyn_inst_seq=9, timestamp=400,
+            commit_status="COMMITTED", trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+            source_event_id=wb,
+            detail=f"consumer_input_fnv={LQ04_HASH}"
+                   f";consumer_input_matches_source=1")
+    w.event("commit", ptag=0, dyn_inst_seq=7, timestamp=350,
+            commit_status="COMMITTED", trace_completeness="COMPLETE")
+    w.close(run_status="COMPLETED", activation="TRIGGERED",
+            terminal="MASKED", completeness="COMPLETE")
+    assert validate(path) == []
+    report = check_completeness(path)
+    # Backward compatibility: propagation_coverage keeps its exact value and
+    # structural_chain_completeness mirrors it.
+    assert report["propagation_coverage"] == "COMPLETE"
+    assert report["structural_chain_completeness"] == \
+        report["propagation_coverage"]
+
+
+def test_run_pilot_wires_fault_effect_fields():
+    # run_pilot imports the classifier the same way it imports the other
+    # platform modules, and the classification block carries the new fields.
+    assert run_pilot.classify is classify
+    src = (PLATFORM / "run_pilot.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    for key in ('"structural_chain_completeness"',
+                '"fault_effect_propagation_coverage"',
+                '"fault_effect_evidence"',
+                '"fault_effect_reason"'):
+        assert key in src

+"""Tests for tools/lsu_propagation_audit.py (LSU v3.2.11 Task 8 / B2+B6+B7+B9).
+
+The fixtures under tests/fixtures/audit/ are synthetic v3.1 JSONL pilots that
+mirror the real trace contract: ``record_type`` event/propagation_edge/summary
+records, semicolon ``detail`` key=value strings, identity fields as JSON
+strings (real vintages mix ints and strings, so the tool must normalize with
+``str()``), a two-line ``oracle.log`` and a ``manifest.json`` carrying the
+recorded binary SHA256.
+
+Coverage required by the task brief and the scope spec:
+
+- a linked LQ04 chain grades ``LIFECYCLE_ONLY`` with ``consumer_verified=yes``
+  (intact payload end to end -- structural completeness is not effect);
+- an adjacent-but-unlinked FW02 pair is never ``COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN``
+  (B3: time adjacency is not causality);
+- a binary SHA mismatch is reported as ``BINARY_SHA_MISMATCH`` and never
+  silently merged (B9);
+- a missing pilot dir yields ``PILOT_DIR_ABSENT`` (never a blank);
+- the matrix writer emits exactly the 20 enumerated columns with no empty cell
+  and no bare ``PASS`` (B7);
+- a malformed JSONL line is counted, not fatal (B2 streaming).
+"""
+import csv
+import json
+import shutil
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / "tools"))
+
+from lsu_propagation_audit import (  # noqa: E402
+    MATRIX_COLUMNS,
+    STAGE_FIELDS,
+    audit_model,
+    audit_pilot,
+    write_matrix,
+)
+
+FIXTURES = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fixtures" / "audit"
+REAL_SHA = "f3c2a4423aab81ca565c7adcfb840a7b8f9cdb9f2a6b6b5ecc3d0e148af20694"
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# audit_pilot
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_linked_lq04_chain_stages_yes_and_lifecycle_only():
+    result = audit_pilot(FIXTURES / "linked_lq04", "LQ04")
+    assert result["stages"]["consumer_verified"] == "yes"
+    assert result["classification"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+    # every one of the nine stages is verified on the intact chain
+    for field in STAGE_FIELDS:
+        assert result["stages"][field] == "yes", field
+    assert result["missing"] == []
+    # evidence bundle carries the required primary facts
+    ev = result["evidence"]
+    assert ev["injection_seq"] == "3"
+    assert ev["pc"] == "400338"
+    assert ev["tick"] == "80080"
+    assert ev["event_ordinal"] == "1"
+    assert ev["earliest_anomaly"]["event_id"] == "8"
+    assert ev["earliest_anomaly"]["event_type"] == "memory_response"
+    assert ev["writeback"]["phys_reg"] == "44"
+    assert ev["writeback"]["writeback_value_fnv"] == "c6d4496e17859d5"
+    assert ev["consumer"]["event_id"] == "18"
+    assert ev["consumer"]["pc"] == "40034c"
+    assert ev["consumer"]["consumer_input_fnv"] == "c6d4496e17859d5"
+    assert ev["commit"]["commit_seq"] == "3"
+    assert result["terminal"]["adjudicated"] == "MASKED"
+    assert result["terminal"]["reason"] == "TARGET_COMMITTED_DATA_VALUE_PRESENT"
+    assert result["oracle"]["expected"] == "b097eb83f71545cb"
+    assert result["oracle"]["actual"] == "b097eb83f71545cb"
+    assert result["oracle"]["pass"] is True
+    assert ev["binary_sha"] == REAL_SHA
+    assert ev["structural_chain_completeness"] == "COMPLETE"
+    assert result["identity"]["sample_run_id"] == "LQ04-F0-W2-S01-R01"
+    assert result["identity"]["seed"] == "1842987246"
+
+
+def test_adjacent_unlinked_is_never_complete_causal_chain():
+    result = audit_pilot(FIXTURES / "adjacent_unlinked", "FW02")
+    assert result["classification"] in ("UNRESOLVED", "PARTIAL")
+    assert result["classification"] == "UNRESOLVED"
+    assert result["classification"] != "COMPLETE_CAUSAL_CHAIN"
+    # the adjacent events carry a different ptag/dyn_inst_seq and no link,
+    # so no downstream stage is verified and every gap has an explicit code
+    assert result["stages"]["consumer_verified"] == "missing"
+    assert result["stages"]["register_writeback_verified"] == "missing"
+    assert result["stages"]["commit_verified"] == "missing"
+    assert "NO_CONSUMER_EDGE" in result["missing"]
+    assert "NO_EARLY_ANOMALY_EVENT" in result["missing"]
+    assert result["missing"]  # never empty when stages are missing
+    assert result["terminal"]["adjudicated"] == "UNRESOLVED"
+
+
+def test_binary_mismatch_reported_not_merged():
+    result = audit_pilot(FIXTURES / "linked_lq04", "LQ04",
+                         binary_sha="deadbeef")
+    assert result["missing"] == ["BINARY_SHA_MISMATCH"]
+    # the effect grade is still derived from trace evidence, but the
+    # provenance failure is an explicit, unmergeable code
+    assert result["classification"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+
+
+def test_binary_sha_match_produces_no_code():
+    result = audit_pilot(FIXTURES / "linked_lq04", "LQ04",
+                         binary_sha=REAL_SHA)
+    assert result["missing"] == []
+
+
+def test_missing_pilot_dir():
+    result = audit_pilot(Path("/nonexistent/audit/XYZ-R99"), "LQ04")
+    assert "PILOT_DIR_ABSENT" in result["missing"]
+    assert result["classification"] == "UNRESOLVED"
+    assert all(v == "missing" for v in result["stages"].values())
+
+
+def test_malformed_line_counted_not_fatal():
+    result = audit_pilot(FIXTURES / "malformed_line", "LQ04")
+    assert result["evidence"]["malformed_records"] == 1
+    assert "MALFORMED_RECORDS" in result["missing"]
+    # the rest of the chain is still evaluated
+    assert result["stages"]["injection_verified"] == "yes"
+    assert result["stages"]["consumer_verified"] == "yes"
+
+
+def test_trace_hash_mismatch_detected(tmp_path):
+    pilot = tmp_path / "LQ04-R04"
+    shutil.copytree(FIXTURES / "linked_lq04", pilot)
+    (pilot / "trace_v31.jsonl.sha256").write_text("00" * 32 + "\n")
+    result = audit_pilot(pilot, "LQ04")
+    assert "HASH_MISMATCH" in result["missing"]
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# audit_model
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_audit_model_single_pilot_row_fields():
+    row = audit_model("LQ04", [FIXTURES / "linked_lq04"])
+    for col in MATRIX_COLUMNS:
+        assert col in row, col
+    assert row["ModelID"] == "LQ04"
+    assert row["PilotCount"] == "1"
+    assert row["InjectionVerified"] == "yes(1/1)"
+    assert row["OracleVerified"] == "yes(1/1)"
+    assert row["StructuralChainCompleteness"] == "COMPLETE(1/1)"
+    assert row["FaultEffectPropagationCoverage"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY(1/1)"
+    assert row["FinalClassification"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+    assert row["MissingEvidence"] == "none"
+    assert row["SupplementaryPilotRequired"] == "0"
+    assert str(FIXTURES / "linked_lq04") in row["EvidencePaths"]
+    assert row["SubUnit"] == "LQ"
+    assert "LQEntry" in row["FaultDefinition"]
+    assert row["binary_splits"] == []
+
+
+def test_audit_model_binary_split_never_merges(tmp_path):
+    other = tmp_path / "LQ04-R02"
+    shutil.copytree(FIXTURES / "linked_lq04", other)
+    manifest = json.loads((other / "manifest.json").read_text())
+    manifest["gem5"]["binary_sha256"] = "cc33" * 16
+    manifest["sample_run_id"] = "LQ04-F0-W2-S01-R02"
+    (other / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2))
+    meta = json.loads((other / "pilot_meta.json").read_text())
+    meta["pilot_id"] = "LQ04-F0-W2-S01-R02"
+    meta["excel_identity"]["sample_run_id"] = "LQ04-F0-W2-S01-R02"
+    (other / "pilot_meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, indent=2))
+
+    result = audit_model("LQ04", [FIXTURES / "linked_lq04", other])
+    assert result["FinalClassification"] == "MIXED_BINARY_SPLIT"
+    splits = result["binary_splits"]
+    assert len(splits) == 2
+    assert {s["ModelID"] for s in splits} == {"LQ04@f3c2a442", "LQ04@cc33cc33"}
+    for split in splits:
+        for col in MATRIX_COLUMNS:
+            assert split.get(col) not in (None, ""), col
+        assert split["FinalClassification"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+    assert result["PilotCount"] == "2"
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# write_matrix (B7)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def _synthetic_row(model):
+    """A well-formed non-LQ04 row; the writer test is about the format."""
+    row = {"ModelID": model, "SubUnit": "TEST", "FaultDefinition": "synthetic",
+           "PilotCount": "1", "FinalClassification": "UNRESOLVED",
+           "MissingEvidence": "NO_INJECTION_EVENT",
+           "SupplementaryPilotRequired": "1",
+           "EvidencePaths": f"/synthetic/{model}-R01",
+           "StructuralChainCompleteness": "INCOMPLETE(1/1)",
+           "FaultEffectPropagationCoverage": "UNRESOLVED(1/1)",
+           "FaultCausality": "n/a(1/1)"}
+    for col in ("InjectionVerified", "ActivationVerified", "EarlyAnomalyVerified",
+                "IntermediatePropagationVerified",
+                "RegisterWritebackVerified", "ConsumerVerified",
+                "CommitVerified", "TerminalVerified", "OracleVerified"):
+        row[col] = "yes(1/1)"
+    return row
+
+
+def test_write_matrix_exact_20_columns_11_rows_no_empty_cells(tmp_path):
+    rows = [_synthetic_row(m) for m in
+            ("A01", "A03", "FW02", "SQ01", "DA04", "LP01", "MD04",
+             "FW05", "SQ03", "SP05")]
+    lq04 = audit_model("LQ04", [FIXTURES / "linked_lq04"])
+    rows.append({col: lq04[col] for col in MATRIX_COLUMNS})
+    out = tmp_path / "matrix.csv"
+    write_matrix(rows, out)
+
+    with open(out, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+        reader = csv.reader(fh)
+        header = next(reader)
+        data = list(reader)
+    expected_header = [
+        "ModelID", "SubUnit", "FaultDefinition", "PilotCount",
+        "InjectionVerified", "ActivationVerified", "EarlyAnomalyVerified",
+        "IntermediatePropagationVerified", "RegisterWritebackVerified",
+        "ConsumerVerified", "CommitVerified", "TerminalVerified",
+        "OracleVerified", "StructuralChainCompleteness",
+        "FaultEffectPropagationCoverage", "FaultCausality",
+        "FinalClassification", "MissingEvidence",
+        "SupplementaryPilotRequired", "EvidencePaths"]
+    assert header == expected_header
+    assert header == MATRIX_COLUMNS
+    assert len(header) == 20
+    assert len(data) == 11
+    for row in data:
+        assert len(row) == 20
+        for cell in row:
+            assert cell.strip() != ""
+            assert cell.strip().upper() != "PASS"
+    # the LQ04 row is the real audit verdict, not a copy of history
+    assert data[10][0] == "LQ04"
+    assert data[10][16] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+    assert data[10][18] == "0"
+
+
+def test_write_matrix_rejects_empty_cell_and_bare_pass(tmp_path):
+    bad = _synthetic_row("A01")
+    bad["SubUnit"] = ""
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        write_matrix([bad], tmp_path / "m1.csv")
+    bad2 = _synthetic_row("A01")
+    bad2["OracleVerified"] = "PASS"
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
+        write_matrix([bad2], tmp_path / "m2.csv")
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# CLI
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_cli_json_and_matrix_outputs(tmp_path, capsys):
+    from lsu_propagation_audit import main
+    rc = main(["--model", "LQ04",
+               "--pilot-dir", str(FIXTURES / "linked_lq04"),
+               "--binary-sha", REAL_SHA,
+               "--json", str(tmp_path / "audit.json"),
+               "--matrix", str(tmp_path / "matrix.csv")])
+    assert rc == 0
+    payload = json.loads((tmp_path / "audit.json").read_text())
+    assert payload["row"]["FinalClassification"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+    assert payload["row"]["MissingEvidence"] == "none"
+    assert payload["pilots"][0]["classification"] == "LIFECYCLE_ONLY"
+    with open(tmp_path / "matrix.csv", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+        rows = list(csv.reader(fh))
+    assert len(rows) == 2  # header + one model row
+    out = capsys.readouterr().out
+    assert "LIFECYCLE_ONLY" in out

+"""v3.2.11 audit additions to the v3.2.6 workbook."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import openpyxl
+import pytest
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+BASE = ROOT / "docs/gem5-fi/lsu/implementation_v3_2"
+V325 = BASE / "excel/gem5-fi-LSU-Final-Paper-Experiment-Plan-v3.2.5.xlsx"
+V326 = BASE / "excel/gem5-fi-LSU-Final-Paper-Experiment-Plan-v3.2.6.xlsx"
+AUDIT_SHEET = "20.v3.2.11传播链审计"
+CHANGED = {("4.源码Hook审计", "G4"), ("7.Trace Hook规范", "D20"),
+           ("11.风险与待确认事项", "F19"), ("11.风险与待确认事项", "F33"),
+           ("11.风险与待确认事项", "F45")}
+
+
+def load():
+    return openpyxl.load_workbook(V325), openpyxl.load_workbook(V326)
+
+
+def test_audit_sheet_and_matrix_rows():
+    _, wb = load()
+    assert AUDIT_SHEET in wb.sheetnames
+    ws = wb[AUDIT_SHEET]
+    header = [c.value for c in ws[1]]
+    assert header == ["ModelID", "SubUnit", "FaultDefinition", "PilotCount",
+                      "InjectionVerified", "ActivationVerified", "EarlyAnomalyVerified",
+                      "IntermediatePropagationVerified", "RegisterWritebackVerified",
+                      "ConsumerVerified", "CommitVerified", "TerminalVerified",
+                      "OracleVerified", "StructuralChainCompleteness",
+                      "FaultEffectPropagationCoverage", "FaultCausality",
+                      "FinalClassification", "MissingEvidence",
+                      "SupplementaryPilotRequired", "EvidencePaths"]
+    models = [ws.cell(r, 1).value for r in range(2, ws.max_row + 1)]
+    assert models == ["A01", "A03", "FW02", "SQ01", "DA04", "LP01", "MD04",
+                      "FW05", "SQ03", "SP05", "LQ04"]
+    for r in range(2, ws.max_row + 1):
+        for c in range(1, 21):
+            v = ws.cell(r, c).value
+            assert v not in (None, "", "PASS"), (r, c, v)
+
+
+def test_notes_and_changelog_present():
+    _, wb = load()
+    ws3 = wb["3.最终12模型矩阵"]
+    notes = {ws3.cell(r, 1).value: (ws3.cell(r, 18).value or "")
+             for r in range(2, ws3.max_row + 1)}
+    for m in ("A01", "A03", "FW02", "SQ01", "DA04", "LP01", "MD04",
+              "FW05", "SQ03", "SP05", "LQ04", "RP01"):
+        assert "v3.2.11 audit:" in notes[m] or "v3.2.11 design:" in notes[m], m
+    ws19 = wb["19.v3.2.11变更记录"]
+    col_a = {ws19.cell(r, 1).value for r in range(1, ws19.max_row + 1)}
+    assert "11-model propagation audit" in col_a
+    assert "RP01 design gate" in col_a
+
+
+def test_identity_and_freeze_invariants():
+    _, wb = load()
+    ws5 = wb["5.60个样本执行矩阵"]
+    ids = [tuple(ws5.cell(r, c).value for c in (1, 2, 3, 4))
+           for r in range(2, 62)]
+    assert len(ids) == 60
+    frozen = {ws5.cell(r, 38).value for r in range(2, 62)}
+    assert frozen == {"NO"}
+    warm = {ws5.cell(r, 23).value for r in range(2, 62)}
+    roi = {ws5.cell(r, 24).value for r in range(2, 62)}
+    assert warm == {"NEEDS_RESEARCHER_CONFIRMATION"}
+    assert roi == {"NEEDS_RESEARCHER_CONFIRMATION"}
+    ws3 = wb["3.最终12模型矩阵"]
+    rp01 = next(r for r in range(2, ws3.max_row + 1)
+                if ws3.cell(r, 1).value == "RP01")
+    assert ws3.cell(rp01, 26).value == "NEEDS_USER_DECISION"
+
+
+def test_no_other_cell_changed():
+    old, new = load()
+    for name in old.sheetnames:
+        if name == "19.v3.2.11变更记录":
+            continue
+        wo, wn = old[name], new[name]
+        for row in wo.iter_rows():
+            for cell in row:
+                coord = cell.coordinate
+                if (name, coord) in CHANGED:
+                    continue
+                if name == "3.最终12模型矩阵" and cell.column == 18:
+                    continue
+                nv = wn[coord].value
+                assert nv == cell.value, (name, coord, cell.value, nv)

+"""v3.2.6 workbook: A2 LQ04 line-number corrections only; all else unchanged.
+
+Excel v3.2.6 is v3.2.5 plus the corrections ordered by
+``docs/gem5-fi/lsu/implementation_v3_2/LQ04_Source_Audit_line_corrections_v3.2.11.md``
+(§5/§6 for sheet ``4.源码Hook审计`` ``G4``, §7.1 for sheet ``7.Trace Hook规范``
+``D20``, §7.2/§7.3 for sheet ``11.风险与待确认事项`` ``F19``/``F33``/``F45``)
+plus the new ``19.v3.2.11变更记录`` sheet. Every other cell must keep its
+v3.2.5 value: the 60 SampleRunID/RunID/SubTaskID/Seed identities,
+ConfigFrozen=NO, Warm-up/ROI NEEDS_RESEARCHER_CONFIRMATION and the RP01
+DecisionStatus NEEDS_USER_DECISION.
+"""
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import openpyxl
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+EXCEL = ROOT / "docs/gem5-fi/lsu/implementation_v3_2/excel"
+V325 = EXCEL / "gem5-fi-LSU-Final-Paper-Experiment-Plan-v3.2.5.xlsx"
+V326 = EXCEL / "gem5-fi-LSU-Final-Paper-Experiment-Plan-v3.2.6.xlsx"
+
+SAMPLE_SHEET = "5.60个样本执行矩阵"
+MODEL_SHEET = "3.最终12模型矩阵"
+HOOK_SHEET = "4.源码Hook审计"
+TRACE_SHEET = "7.Trace Hook规范"
+RISK_SHEET = "11.风险与待确认事项"
+REVISION_SHEET = "19.v3.2.11变更记录"
+AUDIT_SHEET = "20.v3.2.11传播链审计"
+
+OLD_G4 = ("LSQUnit::insertLoad (353-414); commitLoad (775-798); "
+          "squash (986-1020); clear/pop_front")
+NEW_G4 = ("LSQUnit::insertLoad (394-463); executeLoad (719-831, "
+          "LQ04 hook 796-828); lq04Restore (927-943); commitLoad (945-973); "
+          "commitLoads (975-987); squash (1202-1338, "
+          "load-side window 1205-1254)")
+
+OLD_D20 = "lsq_unit.cc:353-414,775-798,986-1020,1376-1727"
+NEW_D20 = ("lsq_unit.cc:394-463,719-831,927-943,945-973,975-987,"
+           "1202-1338,1641-2074")
+
+OLD_F19 = "lsq_unit.cc:775-798,986-1020"
+NEW_F19 = "lsq_unit.cc:945-973,1202-1338"
+OLD_F33 = "lsq_unit.cc:775-798"
+NEW_F33 = "lsq_unit.cc:945-973"
+OLD_F45 = "lsq_unit.hh:107-125; lsq.hh:539"
+NEW_F45 = "lsq_unit.hh:117-140; lsq.hh:539"
+
+# (sheet, coordinate): (v3.2.5 value, v3.2.6 value) -- the only cells that
+# may differ between the two workbooks.
+CHANGED = {
+    (HOOK_SHEET, "G4"): (OLD_G4, NEW_G4),
+    (TRACE_SHEET, "D20"): (OLD_D20, NEW_D20),
+    (RISK_SHEET, "F19"): (OLD_F19, NEW_F19),
+    (RISK_SHEET, "F33"): (OLD_F33, NEW_F33),
+    (RISK_SHEET, "F45"): (OLD_F45, NEW_F45),
+}
+
+IDENTITY_COLS = ("SampleRunID", "RunID", "SubTaskID", "Seed")
+
+# Frozen LQ04 fault-definition fields of sheet 4 row 4 (correction doc §4):
+# TargetField, NormalValue (both), FaultValue (both), InjectionTiming,
+# Duration, plus the accepted DecisionStatus.
+FROZEN_COORDS = ("H4", "K4", "AB4", "L4", "AC4", "M4", "N4", "AG4")
+
+
+def _load(path):
+    return openpyxl.load_workbook(path, data_only=True)
+
+
+def _headers(ws):
+    return {str(c.value).strip(): c.column for c in ws[1] if c.value}
+
+
+def _sample_rows(ws):
+    return [r for r in range(2, ws.max_row + 1) if ws.cell(r, 1).value]
+
+
+def test_v326_is_v325_sheets_plus_revision_sheet():
+    old = _load(V325)
+    new = _load(V326)
+    assert new.sheetnames == old.sheetnames + [REVISION_SHEET, AUDIT_SHEET]
+    assert len(new.sheetnames) == 20
+
+
+def test_60_identity_rows_unchanged():
+    a = _load(V325)[SAMPLE_SHEET]
+    b = _load(V326)[SAMPLE_SHEET]
+    ca, cb = _headers(a), _headers(b)
+    rows = _sample_rows(b)
+    assert len(rows) == 60
+    for r in rows:
+        for name in IDENTITY_COLS:
+            assert a.cell(r, ca[name]).value == b.cell(r, cb[name]).value, \
+                (r, name)
+

+"""No-fault control validity semantics (LSU v3.2.11 Task 4 / A4).
+
+A matched no-fault control (``--probability 0.0``) is a record of absence:
+the injector must not fire, the un-faulted workload must pass its oracle,
+and the trace must still convert cleanly.  Before v3.2.11 the fault-run
+acceptance expression was applied to controls too, so every healthy control
+was written as ``accepted: false`` -- the A4 defect.  These tests pin the
+corrected semantics: controls have their own validity gate
+(``run_roles.control_valid``), their terminal honestly stays ``UNRESOLVED``
+/ ``NO_INJECTION_EVENT`` (never ``MASKED``), their activation status is
+``NOT_TRIGGERED``, and a valid control exits 0.
+
+Fix-loop ruling (reviewed finding, Important): acceptance is role-exclusive
+(``run_roles.compute_acceptance``) -- a CONTROL can never be accepted
+through the fault criteria, so a control whose injector misfired
+(``injection_count > 0``, reachable for ordinal-gated injectors run with
+``--probability 0.0``) is rejected and flagged ``control_misfire: true``
+even when the fault gates would have passed it.  This supersedes the
+brief's literal ``fault_valid or control_valid`` disjunction because a
+control is not a fault run.
+
+The synthetic records below are built through the same helpers ``run_pilot``
+calls (``run_roles``, ``terminal_rules.adjudicate``,
+``run_pilot._lq04_target_proof``); gem5 is never invoked.
+"""
+import inspect
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+PLATFORM = ROOT / "docs/gem5-fi/lsu/implementation_v3_2/platform"
+sys.path.insert(0, str(PLATFORM))
+
+import run_pilot  # noqa: E402
+import run_roles  # noqa: E402
+import terminal_rules  # noqa: E402
+
+PILOTS_V3_2_10 = (ROOT / "docs/gem5-fi/lsu/implementation_v3_2"
+                  / "pilots_v3_2_10")
+
+# A committed, propagated-and-masked LQ04 fault chain, in the v3.1 JSONL
+# event shape terminal_rules.adjudicate consumes.
+FAULT_CHAIN_EVENTS = [
+    {"record_type": "event", "event_type": "injection", "ptag": 7,
+     "dyn_inst_seq": 42, "detail": ""},
+    {"record_type": "event", "event_type": "commit", "ptag": 0,
+     "dyn_inst_seq": 42, "detail": ""},
+    {"record_type": "event", "event_type": "register_writeback", "ptag": 7,
+     "dyn_inst_seq": 42, "detail": "", "new_value": "0xdeadbeef"},
+]
+
+
+def test_classify_run_role_boundaries():
+    assert run_roles.classify_run_role(0.0) == "CONTROL"
+    assert run_roles.classify_run_role(1.0) == "FAULT"
+    # Boundary: any nonzero probability -- however small -- is a fault run.
+    assert run_roles.classify_run_role(1e-9) == "FAULT"
+    assert run_roles.classify_run_role(0.5) == "FAULT"
+    # Boundary: a negative probability is never a control.
+    assert run_roles.classify_run_role(-1e-9) == "FAULT"
+    assert run_roles.classify_run_role(-0.5) == "FAULT"
+    assert run_roles.classify_run_role(-1.0) == "FAULT"
+
+
+def test_control_valid_accepts_clean_zero_injection_control():
+    assert run_roles.control_valid(exit_code=0, oracle_pass=True,
+                                   injection_count=0,
+                                   trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                                   validator_errors=[]) is True
+
+
+def test_control_valid_rejects_nonzero_exit_code():
+    assert run_roles.control_valid(exit_code=1, oracle_pass=True,
+                                   injection_count=0,
+                                   trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                                   validator_errors=[]) is False
+    # A signal-killed gem5 (negative returncode) is not a clean exit.
+    assert run_roles.control_valid(exit_code=-11, oracle_pass=True,
+                                   injection_count=0,
+                                   trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                                   validator_errors=[]) is False
+
+
+def test_control_valid_rejects_oracle_failure():
+    assert run_roles.control_valid(exit_code=0, oracle_pass=False,
+                                   injection_count=0,
+                                   trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                                   validator_errors=[]) is False
+
+
+def test_control_valid_rejects_any_injection():
+    assert run_roles.control_valid(exit_code=0, oracle_pass=True,
+                                   injection_count=1,
+                                   trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                                   validator_errors=[]) is False
+
+
+def test_control_valid_requires_complete_trace():
+    assert run_roles.control_valid(exit_code=0, oracle_pass=True,
+                                   injection_count=0,
+                                   trace_completeness="INCOMPLETE",
+                                   validator_errors=[]) is False
+
+
+def test_control_valid_requires_no_validator_errors():
+    assert run_roles.control_valid(exit_code=0, oracle_pass=True,
+                                   injection_count=0,
+                                   trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                                   validator_errors=["trace missing"]) is False
+
+
+def test_compute_acceptance_is_role_exclusive():
+    # Truth table of the role-exclusive acceptance ruling: a run is judged
+    # only by its own role's gate.
+    # A CONTROL can never be accepted through the fault criteria -- not
+    # even when fault_valid is true (the misfiring-control case).
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("CONTROL", True, False) is False
+    # A valid control is accepted through its own gate.
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("CONTROL", False, True) is True
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("CONTROL", True, True) is True
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("CONTROL", False, False) is False
+    # A FAULT run is accepted only through the fault criteria; the control
+    # gate is not applicable to it.
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("FAULT", True, False) is True
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("FAULT", False, True) is False
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("FAULT", True, True) is True
+    assert run_roles.compute_acceptance("FAULT", False, False) is False
+
+
+def _build_meta(*, probability, exit_code, oracle_pass, injected,
+                trace_completeness, validator_errors,
+                adjudication_events=(), target_proof_injections=()):
+    """Mirror run_pilot.main's acceptance + meta wiring via its helpers.
+
+    The expressions below replicate the v3.2.11 acceptance block (kept in
+    lockstep by test_run_pilot_wires_roles_into_acceptance); the record
+    shape is what run_pilot would write for these inputs.
+    """
+    crash_by_fault = False
+    observed_terminal = "MASKED" if (exit_code == 0 and oracle_pass) else "SDC"
+    activation = "TRIGGERED" if injected else "NOT_TRIGGERED"
+    final_terminal, terminal_reason = terminal_rules.adjudicate(
+        list(adjudication_events), model="LQ04",
+        observed_terminal=observed_terminal, oracle_pass=oracle_pass,
+        trace_complete=(trace_completeness == "COMPLETE"))
+    if final_terminal == "UNRESOLVED" and terminal_reason is None:
+        terminal_reason = "UNRESOLVED_NO_TERMINAL_EVIDENCE"
+    target_proof = run_pilot._lq04_target_proof(
+        list(target_proof_injections), None)
+    run_role = run_roles.classify_run_role(probability)
+    fault_valid = (
+        (exit_code == 0 or crash_by_fault) and injected > 0
+        and target_proof.get("events", 1) > 0
+        and final_terminal not in (None, "UNRESOLVED")
+        and not validator_errors
+        and trace_completeness == "COMPLETE"
+    )
+    control_valid = (
+        run_roles.control_valid(
+            exit_code=exit_code, oracle_pass=oracle_pass,
+            injection_count=injected,
+            trace_completeness=trace_completeness,
+            validator_errors=validator_errors)
+        if run_role == "CONTROL" else False
+    )
+    control_misfire = run_role == "CONTROL" and injected > 0
+    accepted = run_roles.compute_acceptance(run_role, fault_valid,
+                                            control_valid)
+    return {
+        "run_role": run_role,
+        "exit_code": exit_code,
+        "oracle_pass": oracle_pass,
+        "injection_count": injected,
+        "classification": {
+            "fault_activation_status": activation,
+            "observed_terminal": observed_terminal,
+            "terminal_state": final_terminal,
+            "terminal_state_reason": terminal_reason,
+            "trace_completeness": trace_completeness,
+            "oracle_outcome": "PASS" if oracle_pass else "FAIL",
+        },
+        "control_valid": (control_valid if run_role == "CONTROL" else "n/a"),
+        "control_misfire": control_misfire,
+        "accepted": accepted,
+        "process_exit_code": 0 if accepted else 1,
+    }
+
+
+def test_synthetic_control_meta_is_accepted():
+    # The required inputs of a healthy matched no-fault control (the values
+    # the real v3.2.10 LQ04 controls recorded): clean exit, oracle PASS,
+    # zero injections, COMPLETE trace, no validator errors.
+    meta = _build_meta(probability=0.0, exit_code=0, oracle_pass=True,
+                       injected=0, trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                       validator_errors=[])
+    assert meta["run_role"] == "CONTROL"
+    assert meta["control_valid"] is True
+    assert meta["accepted"] is True
+    # The process exit code becomes 0 for a valid control.
+    assert meta["process_exit_code"] == 0
+    # Honesty: the fault gates still reject this record on their own.
+    cls = meta["classification"]
+    assert cls["fault_activation_status"] == "NOT_TRIGGERED"
+    assert cls["oracle_outcome"] == "PASS"
+    # The terminal stays the honest UNRESOLVED/NO_INJECTION_EVENT --
+    # acceptance never relabels a control as MASKED.
+    assert cls["terminal_state"] == "UNRESOLVED"
+    assert cls["terminal_state_reason"] == "NO_INJECTION_EVENT"
+    assert cls["terminal_state"] != "MASKED"
+    # A healthy control did not misfire.
+    assert meta["control_misfire"] is False
+
+
+def test_synthetic_control_meta_with_oracle_failure_is_not_accepted():
+    # An oracle failure on an un-faulted run is an infrastructure problem
+    # (golden mismatch), not SDC and not a valid control.
+    meta = _build_meta(probability=0.0, exit_code=0, oracle_pass=False,
+                       injected=0, trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                       validator_errors=[])
+    assert meta["run_role"] == "CONTROL"
+    assert meta["control_valid"] is False
+    assert meta["accepted"] is False
+    assert meta["process_exit_code"] == 1
+    cls = meta["classification"]
+    assert cls["oracle_outcome"] == "FAIL"
+    assert cls["fault_activation_status"] == "NOT_TRIGGERED"
+    assert cls["terminal_state"] == "UNRESOLVED"
+    assert cls["terminal_state_reason"] == "NO_INJECTION_EVENT"
+    # Zero injections were recorded, so this is not a misfire -- the
+    # control is invalid because its un-faulted oracle failed.
+    assert meta["control_misfire"] is False
+
+
+def test_synthetic_fault_meta_keeps_fault_gates_and_na_control_field():
+    meta = _build_meta(probability=1.0, exit_code=0, oracle_pass=True,
+                       injected=1, trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                       validator_errors=[],
+                       adjudication_events=FAULT_CHAIN_EVENTS,
+                       target_proof_injections=[{"kind": "injection",
+                                                 "ordinal": 1}])
+    assert meta["run_role"] == "FAULT"
+    # Fault runs keep the fault gates; control_valid is reported as n/a.
+    assert meta["control_valid"] == "n/a"
+    assert meta["accepted"] is True
+    assert meta["process_exit_code"] == 0
+    cls = meta["classification"]
+    assert cls["fault_activation_status"] == "TRIGGERED"
+    assert cls["terminal_state"] == "MASKED"
+    # A fault run firing its injector is not a control misfire.
+    assert meta["control_misfire"] is False
+
+
+def test_synthetic_misfiring_control_meta_is_rejected_and_flagged():
+    # The reviewed Important finding: a CONTROL that unexpectedly fired the
+    # injector (reachable for ordinal-gated injectors -- MD04/SQ03/SP05/
+    # FW05 -- run with --probability 0.0). Every ingredient of fault_valid
+    # is present (injection_count 1, adjudicated MASKED terminal on the
+    # fault chain, COMPLETE error-free trace, clean exit), so the brief's
+    # literal disjunction would have accepted it through fault_valid.
+    meta = _build_meta(probability=0.0, exit_code=0, oracle_pass=True,
+                       injected=1, trace_completeness="COMPLETE",
+                       validator_errors=[],
+                       adjudication_events=FAULT_CHAIN_EVENTS,
+                       target_proof_injections=[{"kind": "injection",
+                                                 "ordinal": 1}])
+    assert meta["run_role"] == "CONTROL"
+    cls = meta["classification"]
+    assert cls["fault_activation_status"] == "TRIGGERED"
+    assert cls["terminal_state"] == "MASKED"
+    assert meta["injection_count"] == 1
+    # Role-exclusive acceptance rejects the invalid control outright...
+    assert meta["control_misfire"] is True
+    assert meta["control_valid"] is False
+    assert meta["accepted"] is False
+    assert meta["process_exit_code"] == 1
+
+
+def test_run_pilot_wires_roles_into_acceptance():
+    src = inspect.getsource(run_pilot.main)
+    # The role comes from the CLI probability via the shared classifier.
+    assert "run_roles.classify_run_role(args.probability)" in src
+    # Controls are gated by the shared control predicate.
+    assert "run_roles.control_valid(" in src
+    # Acceptance is role-exclusive: a CONTROL can only pass through the
+    # control gate, a FAULT run only through the fault gate.
+    assert "run_roles.compute_acceptance(" in src
+    # ...never the disjunction of the two gates (reviewed finding).
+    assert "fault_valid or control_valid" not in src
+    # A misfiring control is flagged explicitly in pilot_meta.json.
+    assert '"control_misfire": control_misfire' in src
+    # Every run records its role in pilot_meta.json.
+    assert '"run_role": run_role' in src
+
+
+def test_real_v3_2_10_controls_satisfy_the_control_gate():
+    # Read-only grounding in the frozen v3.2.10 artifacts (never rewritten):
+    # the recorded inputs of every real no-fault control are exactly what
+    # run_roles.control_valid accepts, and the records already carry the
+    # honest control fields.
+    paths = sorted(PILOTS_V3_2_10.glob("LQ04-CONTROL-R*/pilot_meta.json"))
+    assert len(paths) == 5
+    for path in paths:
+        meta = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+        assert run_roles.control_valid(
+            exit_code=meta["exit_code"],
+            oracle_pass=meta["oracle_pass"],
+            injection_count=meta["injection_count"],
+            trace_completeness=meta["classification"]["trace_completeness"],
+            validator_errors=meta["trace"]["validator_errors"],
+        ) is True, path.parent.name
+        cls = meta["classification"]
+        assert cls["fault_activation_status"] == "NOT_TRIGGERED"
+        assert cls["oracle_outcome"] == "PASS"
+        assert cls["terminal_state"] == "UNRESOLVED"
+        assert cls["terminal_state_reason"] == "NO_INJECTION_EVENT"
